## 1 · DISPOSITION + THESIS

**KEEP-THIN structurally · REPAINT materially.** No merge, no split, no new component/composable/file/token-value. One component deleted (`CardAction`), six card-owned props struck + `material` severed, one prop promoted (`selected` standalone).

The card fails on three legs and every cure is in the building: (1) the shell `<Aurora>` field is live and the card cannot transmit it—a 0.65-α veil of a colour 2 HSL-L from its own ground composites to C 0.0158 *under* the ground's 0.0204: the plate dilutes what it claims to transmit; (2) the edge and rim exist in the ladder and the elevated card gets neither—4% ink both arms, and the one attribute that says "I am elevated" deletes the rim wholesale (D1); (3) the focus/hover register ships in `.glass-card`, a class `<Card>` never emits, under a comment asserting the opposite. Meanwhile every off-series value on the route traces to six declarations in one file. The ruled binary: **a transmissive plate that tells the truth about how much it transmits**—veil falls until dilution stops, edge rises to its rung, rim restored by composition, cast unchanged, the impossible +62% refused on arithmetic.

## 3 · §THE DESIGN

**3.1 Rim, restored (D1).** The role seam **composes**: role shadow appends to the rung stack, never substitutes. One edit at `material-roles.css:20-22`, every Surface consumer inherits. Precondition for every white-channel move below. Rim leg values (top 0.30→≤0.12, side leg struck, `::before` plus-lighter ring struck) are W-GLASS-DEDUP's K1/K2/K3; the card gates the result.

**3.2 Field—subtraction, no minted number.** The card mints no veil α (S-6). Gate binds paint: no dilution, floor 0.020, warm hue window. Constraints ride the route to W-BLUR-LADDER/W-FROST: monotone dilution (opaque-rung C 0.0147 < ground 0.0204—every unit of veil lowers composite chroma) and the +40% transmission ceiling (S-7). Blur resting 7→10 direction noted, owned by the ladder wave, landed monotone. `saturate` untouched here—5c rules it innocent and Safari resolves `saturate(1.3) brightness(1.14)` where Chromium resolves `saturate(1.4)` on the same class, so any move adjudicates on the Safari arm first (W-FROST). No `paper-field` revival; no second field; `vividness: 0` capacity → GF-AURORA.

**3.3 Edge—one token, two arms, keyed on `[data-shadow]`.** Elevated (default): ink **0.16** (`--ink-edge`, C9). Flush (`:shadow="false"`): ink **0.08** (`--ink-seam`). Exactly two boundary channels—ink the stronger + one white rim leg ≤0.12 (PROPORTION 5b ruling verbatim). Cast stays `--shadow-card` = `--shadow-md`, the one correctly-scaled leg.

**3.4 Engagement—fold into the register that exists.** `.glass-card` survives (284/275 hit-files—both counts forbid deletion); the selector widens `:where(.glass-card, .card)`, body byte-identical; the false comment corrected. The `selected`-armed card gains exactly: focus-visible = **`outline`** 2px ink/0.48 offset 2px + `0 0 8px` ink/0.15 (A1/K5—`box-shadow` loses to `button/styles.css:98` at (0,4,0)); hover inside `@media (hover:hover)` = `--fill-hover` 0.05 (K13—unbracketed sticks after tap); **selected = `--fill-selected` 0.12 fill carrying the state + accent rim demoted to locator** (C11 + atlas's "ring, not slab" honoured); press = the shipped `.interactive-item` vocabulary, `--scale-press-sm` on the spatial spring leg (S-9). `selected !== undefined` arms `role="option"`, `tabindex="0"`, `aria-selected`, `cursor: pointer`. **Idle breath NOT specified**—R-4 (RECONCILIATION:287) unresolved, both sides canon, hard-blocked behind the owner mark.

**3.5 Proportion—generator → values.** `styles.css:4-8` deletes. `--card-pad` (inline = block, one value) **12**/sm **8** (§4 `r:pad = 16:12`, residue exactly 4); intra-group gap 8/4; group gap 12/8; radius 16 unchanged. Type: `CardTitle` **23.67** · `CardDescription` **16.50** · content **18.608** · shrink pair **38.29→30.10** (ratio preserved to four figures). Size law stated: `sm` and the ≤768 transposition are the same operator and do not compose; type never rides either (C13).

**3.6 Shrink—compositor-safe.** Thresholds on-series: condense 20 down, release 8 up. Font-size/padding flip instantly at the threshold; the glide is `transform: scale()` on the title (origin inline-start) + opacity on the backplate—no layout-property transitions. The `::before` loses `backdrop-filter` (D9), becomes a plain fill of the plate's resolved colour. Description exits via `visibility: hidden` + opacity with `transition-behavior: allow-discrete` + one `aria-live="polite"` line. Literals → `--card-shrink-threshold` on the 12/20 rungs.

**3.7 Public surface, after.**
```ts
export interface CardProps extends Omit<SurfaceProps, "material"> {
    size?: CardSize;        // "sm" | "md" — named sub-range export kept (S-10)
    /** Presence makes the card an option: role, tabindex, aria-selected, states. */
    selected?: boolean;
}
```
`withDefaults` → `{ size: "md", shadow: true }`. Cut: `cartoon`, `grid`, `metal`, `variant`, `dataHue`, `dataHueStrength` + `material` (DAG SEVER-VIA `_shared/surface`, 0 external setters). Types `CardTier`/`CardVariant`/`CardMetal` die. Per-instance rim hue = consumer writes `--glass-accent`. `CardAction` deletes (0 external importers, 1 in-repo site, body = 4 grid placements against a `:has()` fork). `CardFooter` keeps (3 in-repo sites, real body). D21 cured in the rewrite.

**3.8 On-disk.** `card-scroll.css` → `card/scroll.css` + `@layer components` + absorbs `.card-scroll-host` from `base-misc.css:183-187` · `.paper-grid` + PRT arm → `styles/paper.css` · `@utility cartoon-surface` re-homed (COLOCATION) with the exclusivity doc line · `[data-variant]` globals → `.card[data-selected]`-scoped · `tests/components/ui/card/` → `tests/components/card/`, dead-attribute assertions dropped · `FadingScroll`'s 16px feather on `.card-scroll-host`.

**Clean bills, so they are not re-raised:** `useLiquidPress` · `Primitive`/`asChild` · `guardDisabledActivation` · the `svg:not([class*="size-"])` host-sized-icon escape · zero `-webkit-backdrop-filter` in `button/` (not in the prefix-trap seven) · no `light-dark()` inset fragment · the capsule's `:active` snap is correctly gated `:not([data-press-armed])` and is the **chip's only press floor** (`chipVariants.ts:4,6` composes capsule-hover with no tap-squish) — never strike it · type values are inside their clamp's own vw term (the arity, not the value, is the defect).

## 3 · §THE DESIGN — one owner per channel, keyed on one attribute; nothing minted off the canonical series

**3.1 One material owner; the `::before` seat moves one word sideways.** `button/styles.css` declares **no** `backdrop-filter` and **no** bare-token `--glass-capsule-fill` (`:41-43, 48-50` die). `Button.vue:69` swaps `glass-wash` → **`glass-specular-track`** (T-1); `.button` gains `position: relative` is already supplied by the specular-track position group — nothing else needed. Host: `button tap-squish focus-ring` + `glass-capsule glass-specular-track` + `glass-capsule-hover`. One backdrop owner (`glass-capsule.css:64`), one `::before`, symmetric gleam by construction. The `glassMaterial` gate (`Button.vue:46-50`) drops its `tone === "neutral"` condition — D10's gate disagreement dies; a destructive button is glass.

The emphasis ladder, three channels, honestly stated:

| emphasis | material | fill | ink/weight |
|---|---|---|---|
| `primary` | `.glass-capsule .glass-deep` **+ `--glass-depth: var(--glass-depth-content)`** (mandatory — without it the tier map (`deep.css:105-113`) no longer matches and the registered initial 1 paints the 16px menu ceiling; T-adjudicator catch iii) | register default — the warm-floor compose over the floating rung | `--foreground`, **600** (650 dies; A8's state step) |
| `secondary` | `.glass-capsule` | `--glass-capsule-fill` re-pointed to the **warm-floor-preserving compose over the quiet rung** (the knob used as its own comment documents — "buttons' quiet triplet"; the D3 sin was the floor-bypassing bare token, not indirection; exact tinted-pair token name re-derived at execution) | `--foreground`, 500 |
| `quiet` | none | transparent; hover `+ ink/0.05` (re-rung from 8%) | `--muted-foreground` → `--foreground` |
| `text` | none | transparent; pad 4 (floor rung) | `--primary`, underline on hover |

Honest caveat carried from Opus: primary-vs-secondary blur separation is ≈1.75px against `--glass-depth-content: 0.35` — likely sub-JND; the load-bearing channels are **fill α** (floating vs quiet rung) and **weight**, blur corroborating; the blur-ladder re-derivation is PROPORTION 5d's, routed. A wave landing blur alone has not landed emphasis (gated in §5 G2).

**3.2 The ring leaves `box-shadow` and stops rewriting geometry.** `base.css:113-117` inverts to `outline: 2px solid` at the perimeter rung (ink 0.48, `--ink-perimeter`), `outline-offset: 2px`, dropping **both** the `box-shadow` and the `border-radius` writes. Every emphasis×tone cell gets an indicator; none loses its material; dialog ✕/toast action/chip cell stop corner-snapping on Tab; K5's 570 unoccupied outline reservations gain their loudest occupant. **No bloom leg** (§7 R5). `.interactive-item:focus-visible` (same file) follows in the same edit; `--focus-ring-shadow` retirement + the invalid-ring twin route to BAND-A11Y.

**3.3 Tone tints; it never replaces.** `tone?: Extract<Tone, "neutral" | "destructive">` per the sub-range law (`axes.ts:9-19`; `TONES` untouched). `styles.css:75-119` (45 lines) dies. Destructive re-points `--glass-capsule-fill` to the emphasis's own compose **mixed toward `var(--destructive)` at `var(--feedback-tone-strength)`** (T-5 — the one existing knob, 18%, licensed by §1.3's co-carrier clause since ink + perimeter co-carry), takes the perimeter rung (1px ink-0.48 mixed toward the tone) in place of the fourth boundary ink, full-chroma tone ink on the label, no `--shadow-sm` (the capsule rim + lift is the elevation channel). Emphasis stays live under tone (D9 cured by construction): primary-destructive = deep tinted capsule, secondary = calm tinted capsule, quiet/text = chromeless, `--destructive` ink, hover fill ink/0.05. Grammar identity with a destructive Alert is a capture (P5), not a class compose.

**3.4 The box axis — four rungs kept, two pads, one gap pair, three type faces.** (T-3, T-6)

| rung | height (untouched — shared cohort) | pad-inline | gap | type |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `xs` | `--control-h-xs` | 8 | 4 | `--control-text-sm` |
| `sm` | `--control-h-sm` | 8 | 4 | `--control-text-sm` (14.63) |
| `md` | `--control-h-md` | 12 | 8 | `--control-text` (16.50) |
| `lg` | `--control-h-lg` | 12 | 8 | `--type-body` (18.608, the W-SEARCH ruling-2 precedent, 0.6% token delta stated) |

Mobile: one rung down (12→8, 8→4, gap 8→4/4→4); type does not move. `[data-icon-only]` takes the control rung r10 (token name follows K4's rename). `data-control-target` stamps **unconditionally** (T-7). Recorded erratum, routed: PROPORTION §1.1 derives gap 8 from "0.5 × md pad-inline" = 16 — the value its own C1 struck; ruled by the role table here, erratum handed to the PROPORTION owner.

**3.5 Three states that differ.** `Button.vue:42-44` splits: `activationBlocked = loading || disabled`; native `disabled` writes for `disabled` only. **Loading**: focusable, `aria-busy`, activation blocked via `guardDisabledActivation` widened to all hosts; `cursor: progress` becomes reachable and stays; paint = a determinate/indeterminate seam track along the capsule's bottom arc at seam mass 0.08, compositor-only, static under PRM, clock = the MOTION-CANON `transient` row consumed **after** W-MOTION's `orb-drop → transient` rename lands (T-9a — never minted here). **Disabled**: geometry + perimeter at full alpha, ink → 0.45, chroma → 0 (*dim ≠ desaturate*, A4); the blanket `opacity` and `pointer-events: none` die (restoring cursor + `title`). The PRM block (`:171-175`) dies outright; the surface legs survive PRM by the merged list. The `<StatusDot>` pseudo-spinner leaves the demo.

**3.6 Hover and press — every arm answers.** The merged transition list on `.button` (T-2). Channels per carrier: glass arms = gleam (0.14/0.11 via the renamed capsule knob, T-9c) + `scale: 1.015`, now symmetric on leave (T-1); quiet = fill ink/0.05 + ink lift; text = underline + ink; destructive = the tint deepens on the same fill rung. Press: `Button.vue:61-64` passes `el` — the `--motion-weight` coupling arms; the LERP re-keys `.button.glass-specular-track:active::before`; quiet answers on the 0.05→0.12 step while `:active`; text on ink. One drive, three carriers. Press token: `--scale-press: var(--scale-press-sm)` directly; the `--scale-press-btn` alias dies; **`SegmentedTabs.vue:257` re-points its `readToken` to `--scale-press-sm` and drops the `"0.97"` masking-fallback literal** (Fable's catch, confirmed on disk).

**3.7 No idle carrier.** R-4 hard-blocks `W-IDLE-BREATH`; the plate-level proximity gleam is mechanically dead as designed (T-8) and routes with its per-element-writer cure named. Engagement-on-interaction ships in full above.

**3.8 Public surface.** Props: `emphasis?` (4) · `tone?: "neutral" | "destructive"` · `size?` (4, unchanged) · `iconOnly?` · `loading?` · `type?` · `disabled?` · `class?` · PrimitiveProps. Deleted, no alias: `tone: "success" | "warning" | "info"`. Root barrel + `./button` subpath unchanged (types-only change vs `public-surface.spec.ts`). The contract test relocates out of the phantom `tests/components/ui/` hop; its `.cartoon-cast` pin and `data-control-target` assertion both change.

**3.9 The silent break gets a voice.** An `import.meta.env.DEV`-gated **console.error** (T-9b) on `variant` / `size ∈ {icon, default}` in `useAttrs()`, carrying the migration line; prod-stripped. Consumers are owed nothing but marked addenda ([[consumer-updates ruling]]); silence, however, is a masking fallback.

**ADD:** `glass-specular-track` on the host · `.glass-deep` + `--glass-depth: var(--glass-depth-content)` on primary · the quiet-rung fill compose on secondary · the outline ring · the destructive tint + perimeter + full-chroma ink · the loading track + disabled recast · the merged transition list · pad/gap/type re-rungs + mobile transposition · r10 icon corner · unconditional `data-control-target` · `el` on `useLiquidPress` · the quiet press fill-step · the DEV console.error · ~12 lines of law comment in a file that ships zero. **Demo:** a 4×2 emphasis×tone matrix replacing the single hierarchy row · an `as-child` mount in `demo/stories/` (NotFound's is recorded; the gate reads the story surface) · the loading-track specimen · the xs specimen **stays** (T-3).

**Deliberately NOT added:** a configurator (a matrix shows every cell; ECOUTE (b) standardizes existing ones) · `.btn-punch` (§7 R1) · a second spring · a new material · a layout fix (LAYOUT.md §2 is terminal) · the focus bloom leg · warning/info specimens · the material.css cohort edit (§9).

After: **≈273 nonblank, a ~4% increase** — a replacement, not a diet. `styles.css` 152 → ≈157 (code ≈145 + **12 comment lines** in a file shipping zero against a 39.4% library mean); `Button.vue` 104 → ≈110 (loading split, class swap, DEV assertion); `index.ts` 6. Struck in-component ≈55 (tone fork 45 · PRM 5 · disabled opacity/pointer-events 4 · backdrop/fill/rung writes 6 · hops 2, overlaps deduped); added ≈50 (track ~10 · disabled recast 6 · destructive tint 4 · merged list 6 · fill/type/pad re-rungs 10 · icon corner 2 · depth decl 1 · `el` 1 · console.error ~9). The wave's weight in files it does not own, each riding for cause: `utilities/base.css` −3 (focus inversion) · `glass-capsule.css` −8/+2 (dead literal out; the renamed hover knob in) · `property-regs-specular.css` 2 renamed · `scale-paper.css` −1 alias · `tabs/SegmentedTabs.vue` 1 re-point, literal dropped · `glass.css` −1 sentence. ## §0 · TRI-FOLD RULINGS — every FABLE/OPUS split reproduced and ruled; never averaged

The two foreman specs converge on ~90% of the lane: the dispositions, the 20-defect ledger, the de-animation headline, the `.input-pill` strike, one invalid grammar, the size/type/radius/padding token table, the motion register, the recess photometry, the export sequencing, the environment corrections (PROPORTION §7a STRUCK—Safari 26.4 live 12/12; §5d/§5e engine-qualified), and the routed table. The convergent mass is ADOPTED and consolidated below. The nine cells where they split, ruled on evidence:

| # | split | ruling |
|---|---|---|
| **T-1** | **`.glass-defined` on the field**: FABLE composes it (longhands, fill token resolves the recess, "tier identity survives on the edge"); OPUS strikes it from all four shells | **OPUS WINS—composing-to-cancel falsifier.** On disk this seat: `defined.css` gives the field a plate-over-floor `background-image` stack + ink/14% border; FABLE's own C-5 overrides that border to 0.48 and its C-8 re-derives the fill as a recess—**every channel `.glass-defined` contributes is overridden by FABLE's own rulings**, and the plate/floor image stack is a third transmissivity kill under a fill that must resolve host −4% L. The register declares the control material directly. FABLE's longhand-only construction law SURVIVES (convergent—both specs carry it); cross-member tier parity survives inside the ONE register via the routed joint cut, and `.input-pill`/`.control-surface` leave `defined.css:43-44,79-80`'s lists |
| **T-2** | **textarea growth API**: FABLE mints `autosize?: boolean` + 5lh floor scoped `:not([data-autosize])`; OPUS makes growth the register's default, `rows` the floor, no new prop | **OPUS WINS.** `field-sizing: content` unconditional on `[data-kind="textarea"]`; floor = `rows` (default 3—`rows` finally means what it says); ceiling = `--textarea-content-max` declared at `:root` (12lh); `"content"` leaves `TextareaResize` (verified on disk: the 5-member union at `textarea/types.ts:4`), `resize` becomes purely the drag handle, default `"vertical"`. FABLE's boolean REJECTED: default-`false` reproduces the advertised-but-OFF defect FABLE itself diagnosed; default-`true` is a non-knob. Fixed-height remains a one-line CSS opt-out (`--textarea-content-max` pinned). Engine honesty clause: P9 measures `CSS.supports("field-sizing: content")` on BOTH engines; if Safari 26.4 lacks it, the rows-sized box + handle is the primary's own visible floor (not a mask) and the story copy declares the split |
| **T-3** | **coarse size collapse**: FABLE's F9 "at coarse, `--control-floor` collapses the published axis to one value" + G-FIELD-5's story-declares-collapse clause; OPUS's A9 arithmetic refutation | **OPUS WINS—confirmed on disk this seat.** `light-dark.css:17-21` lifts `--ui-scale` to 1.5 AND `--control-floor` to 44 in the SAME coarse block → `max(2.25/2.5/2.75rem × 1.5, 44)` = **54/60/66, three distinct rungs**. The floor binds only under a consumer-dialed `--ui-scale` < 1, which `light-dark.css:13-14` states verbatim. FABLE's collapse clause and its story-copy clause are STRUCK; the coarse π cell survives as measurement, not as collapse documentation |
| **T-4** | **stepper public names**: FABLE folds to one `Step.vue` but preserves `NumberFieldIncrement`/`Decrement` as public names; OPUS ships one public `NumberFieldStep direction=` and deletes both names | **OPUS WINS.** Preserving two names from one implementation costs either two wrapper SFCs (the fold then saves ~8 lines—nothing) or an `h()` factory (indirection). 0 cross-repo consumers in six repos; clean-break house. `DAG-RULINGS:368` lists the `SelectScrollUpButton.vue (direction-parameterised)` fold as a 24-line reduction target—the settled direction is the fold. Accepted cost, recorded: the public name diverges from reka's `NumberFieldIncrement`/`Decrement`; the SFC still wraps the reka primitives internally, switched on `direction` |
| **T-5** | **stepper clearance binding**: FABLE derives `calc(var(--field-control-height) + 0.25rem)`, zero mint; OPUS mints `--number-field-step-h` | **FABLE WINS—verified by construction on disk.** The clearance padding (`number-field/styles.css:27,32`) lives ON `.number-field__input`, the same element that declares `--field-control-height` (`field-control.css:3`, re-pointed per `data-size`); the same-element read resolves, and once `size` threads to input AND steppers both boxes share the one value—self-syncing, D13/F19 dead by construction. OPUS's token REJECTED: it re-publishes per size rung what `--field-control-height` already resolves on the same element—two mechanisms tracking one value, the desync class reborn one level up, and a mint the canonical series does not cover |
| **T-6** | **`InputProps.type` widening**: FABLE +5 (`date, time, datetime-local, month, week`); OPUS +9 (adds `file, color, range, hidden`) | **FABLE WINS.** The union (verified: 6 members at `input/types.ts:22`) is a design fence, exactly like the `number` exclusion OPUS itself preserves: `range` is Slider's geometry, `file`/`color` are non-text chrome the register would paint absurdly, `hidden` never paints. The five temporal values are text-shaped and are a genuine typed capability hole. Union gains five; the fence is stated in the type's comment |

And `:is(:user-invalid, …)` is already the live selector shape at `field-control.css:68`: ONE selector serving three trigger paths (native constraint validation, the `invalid` prop, the bare attr). OPUS's narrowing would silently drop free native-constraint paint. Grammar collapse = kill the 2px arm + the dead pill arm + the pristine-required destructive paint; the live selector stays |

**No silent drops:** every FABLE §0 C-row, §2-§9 row and every OPUS A-row/F-row is either inside the adopted convergent mass, ruled above, or in §7/§9.

## 1 · §THESIS + DISPOSITION

`input` **KEEP-THIN** · `textarea` **KEEP-THIN + `./textarea` minted** · `number-field` **KEEP-THIN** (`DAG-RULINGS:133` row 40 "KEEP · correct-as-is" **OVERTURNED**—14-line vacuous Content sole-child at 5/5 mounts, byte-twin steppers, no size axis, a fourth invalid grammar, ARIA on a role-less div) · `_shared/field` **SPLIT**. Rows 60/61 keep the verb, lose the "correct-as-is" ground. `./forms` retires only behind the §3.5 sequence.

**The lane question—"one field chassis, three semantic shells?"—is RATIFIED as target, REFUTED as description of HEAD.** At HEAD: one chassis that works (`fieldControl.ts`, 47 lines, 4 consumers, zero defects—the lane's only clean artefact), **three surface registers** (`.field-control` live · `.input-pill` dead with better manners, 124 lines, 0 element consumers · `.control-surface`+`.glass-control-edge`), **three size mechanisms** sharing one type name, **three in-lane invalid grammars** (four in the family), and four shells decorated by two different cascade accidents (`.control-surface` wins the defined recipe by import order; `.field-control`'s `border:`/`background:` shorthands at `:12-13` wipe it). The register was re-homed and the move silently dropped its `transition`, its `:hover` answer, and every media query—**a de-animation shipped as a refactor**; the corpse register is the one carrying a clock. Breath-of-life and liquid-weight both fail from one shared file, by omission.

Target: **ONE chassis (`useFieldControlState`, byte-untouched) · ONE surface register (longhand-declared control material, joint with trigger + checkbox) · two geometries (`line`/`block` via `data-kind`) · four semantic shells** (text · multi-line · numeric · token-well). ~60% of the lane's paint defects belong to the glass-token layer, ~15% to the button register—this lane gets thin by strike and gains exactly one motion register.

## 3 · §THE DESIGN

### 3.1 One register, winning by construction
```
.field-control                     ← geometry + material + type + state; LONGHANDS ONLY
  ├ composes .glass-control-edge   ← the keyed 2-stop rim, relocated to styles/glass/, 2→6 members
  └ composes NOTHING else          ← .glass-defined is a plate register and leaves the field (T-1)
```
Laws: **longhand-only** (F1 dies by construction, not import order) · **`box-shadow` appends, never replaces** (rim = resting silhouette; ring lives on `outline`) · **declared once** (`:15`'s hand-copied rim deleted; `glass-defined` struck from the four SFC class lists and from `defined.css:43-44,79-80`—co-owned, routed). The register serves Input, Textarea, NumberFieldInput, TagsInput, Checkbox, SelectTrigger—the joint cut with the binary-triad and select lanes, or the seam re-opens.

### 3.2 Tokens—canonical series only, zero mints beyond `--textarea-content-max`'s `:root` home

|---|---|---|---|
| radius, all three shells | 9999/16/9999 + dialog fork | **`--radius-control` = 10 everywhere**, both dialog contexts; the fork + apologia die as consequence | PROPORTION §4 binding list (pill list omits input—verified `:234`) |
| `--radius-field` | 3 read sites | dies as this lane's rung (textarea→control; token fate W-RADIUS-ROLE's) | §4 |

| fill | `--input-on-glass` α 1.0, +6.2 L | transmissive, resolves **host −4% L**; readonly one step further down | A10; the α + `material.css:66` kills are W-FROST's, sequenced |
| type | `--control-text` × `--ui-scale` | **rung −1 = 16.50, clamp only**; font invariant across sizes AND `--ui-scale` (lane re-points now; shared token routed) | §1.1/§1.4 (verified `:123`) |

### 3.3 Textarea (T-2)
`field-sizing: content` unconditional on `[data-kind="textarea"]` · floor = `rows` (default 3) · ceiling `max-block-size: var(--textarea-content-max)` declared at `:root` = 12lh · `TextareaResize` narrows to `"vertical"|"horizontal"|"both"|"none"` (drag handle only, default vertical) · net API: one enum member out, zero props in. P9's `CSS.supports` cell on both engines makes the story state the engine truth.

### 3.4 Motion—the register the lane has never had (springs consumed, never minted; verify pairs on disk at implementation)
| rung | channel | clock |
|---|---|---|
| HOVER | well deepens (ink inset 0.06→0.10) + one blur rung, `@media (hover:hover)` | `--spring-press` 0.12s |
| FOCUS BLOOM | `outline-color` 0→0.48 + halo, transform-free, rim preserved | 0.12s |
| INVALID ARRIVE | ring blooms, `--ease-out`, **no overshoot—an error must not bounce** | 0.20s |
| STEP | value reel via `useAnimatedNumber` (T-8; survival re-verified at implementation); held-repeat paints sustained `data-pressed` on reka's auto-repeat ramp | `--spring-press` |
| REST | material only—no idle loop, fenced on R-4; if the owner rules otherwise the idle arm is `BJ.W-IDLE-BREATH`'s, never here | — |

`prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` arm mandatory and new, gate-paired so the reduce arm alone cannot pass on a dead tree.

### 3.5 Number-field + public surface
```
NumberField (.number-field IS the grid; wrapper div + Content die; size?: ControlSize, default md, threaded to input AND steppers)
  ├ NumberFieldStep direction="decrement"   ← ONE public name (T-4); wraps the reka primitives switched on direction
  ├ NumberFieldInput                        ← aria-invalid/required HERE only (spinbutton), off the root div
  └ NumberFieldStep direction="increment"   ← emphasis="secondary" OR W-BUTTON's quiet ring—either discharges G-F5
```
`NumberFieldContent`/`NumberFieldIncrement`/`NumberFieldDecrement` retire—clean break, no alias, 0 cross-repo consumers (six-repo greps, both benches + spot-checked). On disk: `components/_shared/field/{control.css, control.ts}` (module-name strip) · `field-surfaces.css` exits per T-9 · `useVModel`→`defineModel` ×2, `defaultValue` DELETED (3 demo sites, 0 cross-repo)—the vueuse quarantine loses its cargo · `nativeProps` die by undeclaration · `type` union +5 with the fence comment (T-6) · exports: **mint `./input` + `./textarea`, root barrel gains `Input`/`Textarea` + re-homed `ControlSize`, THEN `./forms` + `src/forms.ts` retire** (net 72→73; marked addenda owed to slides ×1 + keyframes.js ×5 per the consumer-updates ruling) · demo: `/forms/textarea` manifest route + cels (coarse, dialog-nested, `rows="3"`, `size` ×3 on NumberField, a populated required-numeric specimen curing F19, an invalid specimen per shell).

## 3 · §THE DESIGN

### 3.1 Shape

```
src/styles/glass/control-bit.css   THE REGISTER (~75 lines) — @import'ed once (boundary law 3)
src/components/_shared/control.ts  ControlSize · controlSizeClass · ControlProps · controlStateAttrs
                                   (renamed from control-size.ts — module-name stripping)
src/components/checkbox/           Checkbox.vue · styles.css (~8) · index.ts
src/components/switch/             Switch.vue · styles.css (~36) · index.ts
src/components/radio-group/        RadioGroup.vue · RadioGroupItem.vue · styles.css (~14) · index.ts
```

Per-shell sheets stay on `<style src>` (boundary law 2); only the register lives in `styles/`. Every shell renders one shape — **the host is the seat, in flow; the face is the paint; the mark is the state**:

```html
<XRoot data-slot="…" class="control-bit tap-squish" v-bind="controlStateAttrs(invalid)">
  <span class="control-bit__face" aria-hidden="true">
    <XIndicator force-mount class="control-bit__mark" aria-hidden="true">…</XIndicator>
  </span>
</XRoot>
```

`.checkbox__seat` dies; the radio's negative margins and gap derivation die; the switch's construction generalises. `getBoundingClientRect` reads 44×44 honestly on all three; overlap is impossible by construction; the radio group's shipped 44px pitch is preserved at gap 0.

### 3.2 The register — one answer per law

- **Object:** `--control-bit-size: 1.25rem` (20 — ADJ-2: tick law exact, on-series, seat residue 12, one size across face and thumb). Face defaults `inline/block-size: var(--control-bit-size)`; `--control-bit-radius: var(--radius-pill)` default.
- **Target:** host `inline-size: max(var(--touch-target), var(--control-bit-face-inline, var(--control-bit-size)))`, `block-size: var(--touch-target)` minimum, `display: inline-grid; place-items: center; flex: none`, `touch-action: manipulation`, in flow. No press or transition declared on the host — `.tap-squish` is composed (ADJ-4); scale inherits to the painted face; MOTION-CANON item 6 retimes the register itself.
- **Face:** `border: 1px solid var(--ink-perimeter)` (0.48 → 3:1; `--control-ring` dies with its 2 readers) · `background: var(--control-recess)` (A10 host − 4% L, token routed §9) · resting rim `box-shadow: var(--glass-rim-top), var(--glass-rim-bottom)` (composed as tokens — the recipe the frost wave owns; `.glass-control-edge` drops to 1 honest consumer) · `transition: background-color/border-color var(--duration-fast) var(--ease-standard)` — face-element-only, no host clobber possible. Checked: `border-color`+`background` → `--control-checked-bg` (3 readers, all here; kept). **No `backdrop-filter` anywhere in the lane** — blur(7px) on a 20px box and blur(1px) on a track both die (K9); the bit reads by ink and fill.
- **Engagement (D8 cure, K13-guarded):** `@media (hover: hover) { .control-bit:hover .control-bit__face { background: var(--fill-hover) } }`; `:active` face → `--fill-selected` — the press answers on fill because 4% of 20px is 0.8px (D-N2).
- **Focus (A1 verbatim, on the face):** host `outline: none`; face `outline: 2px solid var(--ink-perimeter); outline-offset: 2px; box-shadow: var(--glass-rim-top), var(--glass-rim-bottom), 0 0 8px color-mix(in srgb, var(--foreground) 15%, transparent)` — one rule, in one file, rim legs byte-identical to rest (gated). Invalid recolors both via `outline-color/border-color: var(--destructive)`.
- **Invalid (K26, one grammar):** `:is(.control-bit[data-invalid], [data-invalid] .control-bit) .control-bit__face { border-color: var(--destructive); outline-color: var(--destructive) }` — the ancestor arm serves RadioGroup's root `invalid` with zero threading. The other three grammars and `--invalid-ring` delivery die.
- **Disabled (A4):** `cursor: not-allowed`; `--control-bit-mark-ink` and `--control-checked-bg` → `color-mix(in oklab, … 45%, transparent)`; geometry and border at full alpha; `opacity` retires as the channel.
- **Mark (D4 cure):** always mounted (`force-mount`), `scale: 0.6; opacity: 0` → `1/1` under `[data-state="checked"], [data-state="indeterminate"]`; `scale` on `--spring-press`/`--spring-press-duration` (0.12s), `opacity` on `--duration-fast`. Compositor-only. PRM: transition opacity only, scale 1 (one arm; radio's hand-rolled arm dies).
- **One `forced-colors` block** (three today), carrying the Canvas/Highlight fills and the Highlight outline.

| **switch** | face vars `2.75rem × 1.5rem` — **geometry byte-kept (ADJ-1)**; pad `0.0625rem` (**it is the law**); thumb `inline/block-size: var(--control-bit-size)` (20 — the one object; ≡ face-block − 2·(pad+border)); `translate: calc(var(--control-bit-face-inline) − var(--control-bit-face-block))` = travel 20 = thumb Ø, + RTL arm, on `transition: translate var(--spring-dock-duration) var(--spring-dock)` (clock 0.44s → 0.22s on-disk, canon retunes to 0.21); thumb sheds the struck `--glass-highlight` leg (PROPORTION §5b), keeps `--shadow-md`; checked fill rides the register's face transition (the 0s snap dies) |
| **radio-group** | `--radius-pill` spelled once (`50%` ×3 dies); the 8px dot; root composes orientation flex-flow at gap 0 (pitch 44 preserved); roving-focus axis (`orientation`/`dir`/`loop`) — genuinely role-specific |

The thumb is the switch's mark and keeps its translate channel; it does not compose `__mark`. Until then the switch carries honest travel on a correct clock.

### 3.4 Public surface

```ts
// _shared/control.ts
export type ControlSize = "sm" | "md" | "lg";                    // unchanged — the pills'

export interface ControlProps { invalid?: boolean; disabled?: boolean }
export const controlStateAttrs = (invalid?: boolean) =>
  ({ "data-invalid": invalid || undefined, "aria-invalid": invalid || undefined });

CheckboxProps   = PrimitiveProps & FormFieldProps & ControlProps
                & { modelValue?: CheckedState; defaultValue?: CheckedState; value?: SelectionValue; id?; class? }
SwitchProps     = ditto, boolean model    // gains PrimitiveProps + asChild; LOSES size and | null; value → SelectionValue
RadioGroupProps = ditto, SelectionValue model + { orientation?; dir?; loop? }   // loses the throw (ADJ-5)
RadioGroupItemProps gains PrimitiveProps
```

`| null` struck ×3 (an optional prop is already absent-able); `checkedState` struck; the throw + `useForwardProps` fork struck — all three narrow reka's emit by declared emit type through `useForwardPropsEmits`, the pattern two of three already ship. `size` leaves Switch (both arms; 0 of ~12 external sites pass it — verified; role radius is fixed by role, and `--control-h-*` resolve 36/40/44 at desktop, refuting the "all 44" sub-claim — `sizing.css:63-67` verified). Subpaths `./checkbox` and `./radio-group` mint.

## 1 · DISPOSITION + THESIS

**KEEP the name · RE-SEAT the silhouette · ENGINE-ADOPT via an item registry · the reka boundary and all group paint die.**

ToggleGroup is the library's **selection ROW** — an unbounded, wrapping row of individually bounded pills; SegmentedTabs is the **segmented CONTROL** (bounded strip, glide mark, `defineModel<string>` — no multiple arm, ever: `SegmentedTabs.vue:147-149`, adj-V). Today the component has the seam backwards: it keys *material* off cardinality, painting a byte-copy of tabs' recessed capsule well for `single` and nothing for `multiple`, announces every one-of-N chooser as N independent toggle buttons, clips 59% of its own demo strip under copy promising the opposite, and paints its selected state as shadcn's opaque `--accent` slab. **17 of 24 mounts in the world pay bytes to fight it; one consumer de-pills it by hand and calls that "the SAME de-pill move O-C6 will make platform-wide"; another names the posture "lettered to glass-ui."** The library already wrote the cure — `useSelectionGroup`: role-per-mode ARIA, the ONE roving machine, the recenter, and an `indicatorRef`-omitted contract that names "a plain toggle row" as a first-class caller — and never wired it. The cut adopts the engine, deletes every painted byte the group owns, composes the DAG 4.12 chassis instead of copying its registers, and lets the row wrap. Nine of the eleven false comments about this component become true; the two indicator clauses are struck at their owning waves.

## 3 · §THE DESIGN

**3.1 The gestalt cut — the group stops painting.** One change dissolves D-1, D-3, D-5, D-7, D-8 and half of D-9:

```css
.toggle-group {
    display: flex;              /* was inline-flex */
    flex-wrap: wrap;            /* THE declaration — retires 17 consumer overrides */
    inline-size: fit-content;
    max-inline-size: 100%;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;                   /* 4px at ≤768 — literal, marked seam; re-points at the token wave (TF-1) */
}
.toggle-group[data-orientation="vertical"] { flex-direction: column; align-items: stretch; }
```

No background, no backdrop-filter, no rim, no recess, no corner, no padding — both cardinalities, both orientations. `safe center`, `scroll-padding-inline`, `overscroll-behavior-inline`, `vertical-align: middle` die with the case they served. A consumer wanting a plate mints a `Card`/`Surface` at r16 in its own tree — the answer UF-A1 asked for and 17 mounts already hand-roll.

**3.2 DOM.**

```
<div class="toggle-group" :role="groupRole" :data-type :data-size
     [data-orientation] [data-invalid] [aria-invalid] @keydown>
    <button class="toggle-group__item control-surface glass-control-edge
                   glass-capsule-hover tap-squish focus-ring"
            v-bind="itemAttrs(value)" :tabindex="rovingTabindex(idx)"> … </button> × N
</div>
```

reka retires from this component entirely; `inputValue`/`outputValue` (30 lines of paint-time `TypeError` policing an untyped round-trip) die with the boundary; `defineModel` replaces the emit pair (the SegmentedTabs idiom). The item class list is the **DAG 4.12 chassis quartet** (`control-surface glass-control-edge focus-ring tap-squish` — the `Checkbox.vue:37` precedent, TF-9) plus `glass-capsule-hover` for the specular lift.

**3.3 Engine + registry.** `useSelectionGroup` requires `options`/`buttonRefs`/`containerRef`, all non-optional and index-aligned — a slot-projected compound has none, so `toggleGroupContext.ts` → **`context.ts`** re-purposes from a variant/size relay into the **item registry**: each item registers `{value, disabled, el}` on mount, unregisters on unmount, DOM-ordered. That feeds the engine (~+15 lines, the price of adoption, paid once):

```ts
const selection = useSelectionGroup({
    options, buttonRefs, containerRef,        // ← the registry
    model,                                    // ← defineModel
    mode:       computed(() => props.type),
    vertical:   computed(() => props.orientation === "vertical"),
    activation: computed(() =>                // TF-2 — mode-derived, never flat
        props.type === "single" ? "automatic" : "manual"),
    // indicatorRef DELIBERATELY OMITTED — the engine's own "plain toggle row"
    // contract (useSelectionGroup.ts:66-71); squish/measure no-op.
});
```

What arrives: role-per-mode ARIA (`radiogroup`/`radio`/`aria-checked` single; `group`/`aria-pressed` multiple), the ONE roving machine (exactly-one-tabstop, axis-derived arrows, Home/End, wrap, disabled-skip), and the `scrollIntoView` recenter — zero bytes authored, still serving scrolling ancestors. `data-orientation` is stamped by this component from its own prop — D-13 impossible by construction. Single mode never empties the model (`select()` adj-V) — slides' empty-payload re-pin guard becomes obsolete.

**3.4 Public surface.** `ToggleGroup`: `type` (required) · `modelValue` via `defineModel` · `disabled` · `orientation` · `size?: ControlSize` (imported; `ToggleGroupSize` deleted, no alias — TF-7) · `invalid` (→ `data-invalid` + `aria-invalid`, the Switch arm byte-for-byte; lands PROPORTION 7b#2) · `class`. `ToggleGroupItem`: `value` · `disabled` · `class`. **Deleted:** `variant` (+`ToggleGroupVariant`), `rovingFocus`, `loop`, `dir`, `name`, `required`, `defaultValue`, the `FormFieldProps` extension, item-level `variant`/`size`, `ToggleGroupSize`, `ToggleGroupValue`, `ToggleGroupSlotProps`, `ToggleGroupEmits`, `TOGGLE_GROUP_KEY`, the slot payload. `index.ts` → 4 exports. Clean break, no aliases.

**3.5 Tokens — every value on the series or by named law.**

| what | value | law |
|---|---|---|
| group paint | none | ruling; §4 cell rung legal only with no silhouette |
| group gap | 8 → 4 coarse/≤768 | §1.1 member rung; ONE ladder transposition; literal + marked seam (TF-1) |
| item radius | `--radius-pill`, one spelling | §4 pill; retires all 32 `10003px` with one deletion |
| item box | `--control-h-{sm,md,lg}` | 40 fine · 60 coarse, unchanged |
| item pad-inline | 8 · 12 · 20 | §1.1; C1 strikes the 10 |
| item rest material | chassis composed (3.2) | zero copied bytes; border value is the register's — this file authors **zero** border values (both foremen, ratified) |
| selected fill | 0.12 · hover 0.05 · both 0.16 — literals, marked seam | A8/§1.3; ink + weight co-carry so 0.12 is licensed; re-points to `--fill-selected`/`--fill-hover` when the token wave lands |
| selected ink · weight | `--foreground` (A8's token, TF-4) · **600/500** | A8 both halves |
| selected border/shadow | register border unchanged · **no shadow** | D-9 — `box-shadow` is the ring's property |
| focus | inherited `outline` ring | A1/K5 library-wide; no local fork |
| press | `--scale-press: var(--scale-press-sm)` survives | F-3 reconciliation |

**3.6 Motion + breath — by composition, zero new machinery.** State commit = effects cross-fade on `--duration-fast` via `.tap-squish` (MOTION-CANON P1). Hover = `.glass-capsule-hover`: specular 0.14 + scale 1.015 on `--ease-cartoon-punch` — liquid weight with inertia, PRM-zeroed in the register. Press 0.97, PRM-zeroed. **No traveling mark** (the glide belongs to the bounded strip; a glide across a wrapped 3-row field is not a segmented control) — the C-3/W-SPRING-RETUNE clock dependency dissolves. **No idle breath minted** — RECONCILIATION §6 R-4 is owner-only and hard-blocks it; recorded, never laundered. Engagement, concretely: per-item pointer answer (specular + lift), one-widget keyboard answer (mode-correct activation per TF-2), wrap instead of hiding, recenter past the fold. States: rest · hover · press · selected · disabled · focus-visible · invalid · wrapped-multi-row.

**3.7 Demo (`demo/stories/forms/toggle.vue`, 82 → ≈92).** The strip section, its false blurb (:59) and overflow class (:64) die; replaced by a **natural-wrap specimen** at constrained width. An **invalid specimen** lands (PROPORTION 7b#2) and a **disabled** one (7b#4 beneficiary). The two bare `<section>`s (:17, :41) become `StorySection` (LAYOUT §1). The two demo overrides retire — `glass-panel.vue:54`, `AuroraColorSection.vue:184` — receipt at P9.

**STRIKE:** the `[data-type=single]` track block whole (`styles.css:13-23`: bg · backdrop-filter · 3-leg shadow · the `--tab-track-recess-ink` reach · pad 4 · `safe center` · the `10003px`) · `scroll-padding-inline` + `overscroll-behavior-inline` + `vertical-align` (:8-10) · the outline and hover blocks (:68-77 → composed class names) · on-state opaque `--accent` fill, `--primary` 28% border arm, `--shadow-sm` leg (:80-83) · `border: 1px solid transparent` (:41) · pad-sm 10 · the `variant` axis · `rovingFocus`/`loop`/`dir`/`name`/`required`/`defaultValue`/`FormFieldProps` · item `variant`/`size` · the reka imports and every forwarded binding · `inputValue`/`outputValue` · the emit pair · six dead types + the key · the story strip section, blurb, overflow class · both demo wrap overrides.

**ADD:** `flex-wrap: wrap` — one declaration retiring 17 consumer overrides and the 59% clip · role-per-mode ARIA from `itemAttrs()` · the ONE roving machine + recenter · **mode-derived activation** (TF-2) · the item registry (context.ts, ~+15) · the chassis composition incl. `glass-control-edge` (TF-9) · `invalid` (prop + two attributes + a minimal destructive arm on the one-invalid-grammar) · weight-600 arm · selected fill literals with marked seams · rationale comments the component never had (~15 lines: the row-vs-strip seam, the `indicatorRef`-omitted contract, the registry's reason). **Net new machinery: one registry.**

## 3 · §THE DESIGN

**3.1 The frame law + the excursion clause.** `viewBox = "-0.1 -0.1 1.2 1.2"`—square, constant, both modes, every preset, **every frame of a drag**. The bleed is the shipped `VIEW_PAD`; the 30-preset span `y ∈ [−0.0969, +1.0927]` makes it the tight bound—no value minted. Six defects die in the declaration (letterbox 0 by aspect identity; comparability by construction; drag-invariant CTM; the fit machinery dies; SVG-unit axis text dies; `aspect-ratio: 1` becomes true). Handles outside pin to the frame edge **along their own leader** while the value continues (the Slider's rail grammar); `MAX_OVERSHOOT` stays 0.6, no preset breaks, value.js untouched. **The excursion clause (adopted from Opus, threshold corrected):** the curve's extremum is computed **analytically** (`B′(t)=0` is a quadratic—exact, no sampling); when it leaves `[−0.1, 1.1]` (fires only with both handles above **y ≈ 1.2**; worst case 1.3692 at 1.6/1.6), `data-curve-clipped` mounts on the root and the crossed frame edge takes the accent tone. **The excursion is never silent, and no dimension moves.**

**3.2 The split.** `EasingCurve.vue` ~95 (one `<svg role="img">` + `aria-label`: frame, grid, diagonal, n strokes `{css?, fn, tone?}[]`, endpoints, optional travel dot, `progress?`, `label?`; zero interactive descendants, zero composable, zero state; published on `./easing`) · `EasingPicker.vue` ~265 (composes `EasingCurve` + handle overlay + pointer/keyboard authoring + one control row + `v-model`) · `usePicker.ts` ~270 (symbol unchanged) · `EasingConfigurator.vue` **DELETED** · `composables/` **DELETED**. On disk after: `easing/{EasingCurve.vue, EasingPicker.vue, usePicker.ts, constants.ts, index.ts, README.md}`. No `<style>` block minted—Tailwind-first. Incidental truth worth one line: the display svg's `role="img"` re-matches the very selector value.js lost at 7.0.0—but the addendum stays owed regardless (§9); the regression is live today.

**3.3 The page—one editor, two strokes, zero residue.** One `EasingCurve`, both curves overplotted (active full ink, ghost 0.30); a `bezier | steps` segmented control selects which stroke the handles edit; one 40px height-invariant control row `[bezier|steps] · preset ▾ · the literal chip ⧉ · ▶`. The literal chip **is** the copy control—**exactly one print** (from three). Two panels become one: **E4 has no trigger left, E3's residue has nowhere to be.** `<SelectItem value="custom" v-if="preset === CUSTOM_PRESET">Custom</SelectItem>` closes E11 in one line. One column; `EasingPicker.vue:327`'s `lg:` **is deleted, not re-keyed** (LAYOUT kill #14 loses a cited site—bidirectional duty, §9); any future wide arm keys `@container (inline-size >= var(--measure-wide))`. The story mounts `<EasingPicker>` directly—the library's first in-repo mount. This wave owns the §Authoring boundary section only.

**3.4 Unit discipline.** Stroke ladder {frame/grid **1** · leader **1** dashed · handle **2** · curve **3**} CSS px, all `vector-effect: non-scaling-stroke`. Axis type leaves SVG user space; remaining chrome type on the −4 rung (11.50). Hit radii in **px, floor 44px diameter at every clamp position**. Ink: frame/grid at seam 0.08, leaders at edge 0.16; accent chain `--easing-curve-accent: var(--motion-accent, var(--viz-legendre))` **survives byte-for-byte** (§0 ruling). Ghost at 0.30. Space: plate pad 12, control-row gap 8, group gap 12; `gap-4`/`gap-6` die.

**3.5 Material, motion, a11y, engagement.** **`surface?: "card" | "bare"`** (default `"card"`; `"bare"` renders no plate)—retires value.js Law 2 and its forbidden prefix pair; sizing in overridable CSS, never inline (kills the `!important` tax and retires Law 3 with the constant frame); `container-type: inline-size` on the picker root, canvas inline-size-driven, `aspect-ratio: 1` now true. Props 7→**4**: `initial?: Partial<EasingPickerValue>` · `playback?` · `label?` · `surface?`. Published seam `data-slot`; all 8 `data-testid` die. Draw-on `stroke-dashoffset` sweep on preset/mode change; `tap-squish` + `grab`/`grabbing`; travel dot with fading trail (fade, not `v-if` cut); A1 focus grammar on every focusable (`outline: 2px @ ink 0.48` + `0 0 8px @ 0.15`, offset 2), zero `focus:outline-none` without replacement; PRM arm survives **byte-for-byte**, the three new clocks one-frame under `reduce`; **one transport vocabulary** `Preview`/`Replay`/`Cancel`, status region speaks the same three, mounted at idle; handles `role="slider"` with `aria-valuemin/max/now` re-keyed to the y band (−0.6..1.6—the band the shipped sr-only text already speaks) + two-axis `aria-valuetext` + `PageUp`/`PageDown`; the curve svg `role="img"` + label. Breath is a floor, not an idle loop. Staircase analytic: n risers + n+1 treads, ≤25 commands, exact riser x, `stroke-linejoin: miter`.

## 1 · DISPOSITION

**ONE-AXIS RESHAPE + register collapse.** `Surface` keeps its name and element and exactly three knobs — `tier`, `surface`, `deep` — losing `material`, `shadow`, `grain`, `specular` and five of eight emitted attributes. The one resolver relocates to `_shared/surface/resolve.ts` as a plain function; `overlayContentAttrs()` composes it. The register collapses **22 → 17 files**, `.glass-material` is struck from its 18 dead positions, `.glass-card` folds onto `.glass-resting` (with two consumer addenda owed), the duplicate specular source dies behind a byte-parity π, and the plate primitive gains the three things a material must have and this one lacks: **a corner (`--radius-ctx`, zero-mint), a clock (`--spring-press`), and an honest namespace**. The headline restates honestly before any blur is repaired: **188/305 → 149/266 = 56.0%** on the `.track-well` namespace correction alone, → **117/266 = 44.0%** residual once the 32 fill-defect rows are booked to the field lane. The library-defect share of the blur indictment is two one-line causes (`material.css:66`, `button/styles.css:43` — routed); the chassis share is `ShowcaseFrame.vue` deriving `surface="opaque"` around 118 mounts.

## 3 · §THE DESIGN

**3.1 One axis, three props, one plain resolver.**

```ts
// src/components/_shared/surface/resolve.ts — the ONE resolver (module-name stripped)
export function surfaceClass(tier: SurfaceTier, deep = false): string {
    return deep ? `glass-${tier} glass-deep` : `glass-${tier}`;
}

// src/components/surface/Surface.vue — ~35 lines, the whole public surface
interface SurfaceProps extends Omit<PrimitiveProps, "asChild"> {
    tier?: SurfaceTier;      // wash|quiet|resting|floating|overlay — the ONE prominence axis (default "resting")
    surface?: Surface;       // glass|veil|opaque — decoration, orthogonal, unchanged
    deep?: boolean;          // thickness ON the resolved tier — never a tier jump
    class?: HTMLAttributes["class"];
}
// emits exactly: class · style · data-slot · data-surface
```

## 1 · DISPOSITION + THESIS

| member | DAG prior | TERMINAL | one-line ground |
|---|---|---|---|
| badge | KEEP-THIN | **KEEP + RESHAPE, `surface` axis DELETED** | truth-in-DOM, one edge mechanism, annotation type register, `variants.ts` |
| separator | KEEP correct-as-is | **OVERTURNED → KEEP + FIX + INK-FOLD** | 1×0 vertical (C), 1px labelled host (C, new), five inks → one ladder |
| label | KEEP correct-as-is | **OVERTURNED → KEEP + RE-RUNG** | ships the control-VALUE rung as its label; 0.887 lands by construction; K26 dies |
| avatar | KEEP correct-as-is | **OVERTURNED → KEEP + DE-GLASS + DIET + φ-LADDER** | layer leak paints two md type sizes on one page; dead public surface six repos deep; coarse collapse impossible after |
| skeleton | KEEP | **OVERTURNED → KEEP + MECHANISM SWAP (F24 closes)** | both prior diagnoses wrong; the width-dependent sweep dies; a width-invariant breathe replaces it |
| status-dot | KEEP-THIN | **SUSTAINED → KEEP + STATE FIX + BARREL + RELAY** | `active` gets its rule, its orbit, and a neutral unhandled default; MIGRATION rows written after two majors |

DAG rows 41/51/56/58 OVERTURNED in ground ("correct-as-is" is false on paint evidence at HEAD); rows 39/59 SUSTAINED and widened. **No member deleted**—census re-verified this seat with all siblings present: Badge 5 ext/3 repos · StatusDot **7/3** · Separator 3/1 · AvatarImage 1/1 · Label 0 ext (3 src + 27 demo) · Skeleton 0 ext (1 src `DataTable.vue` + 12 demo). value.js is a false zero (its own `demo/ui/` shadow set). keyframes.js consumes through an **undeclared** dependency.

**THE THESIS—one register, six spellings.** These six are the library's entire inert-mark register: non-interactive atoms that annotate a neighbour. Measured: zero `:hover/:focus/:active/transition` rules, zero `backdrop-filter`—the right answer, reached today by omission. The wave makes it law: a mark takes `backdrop-filter: none` as a stated rule (the one glass claim that is engine-invariant, per EXEC-STATE's sixth-Ecoute (d) no-engine-conditional-glass), one divider ink at three ruled alphas, one stadium spelling, sizes derived from the type or control it annotates—never a literal—and exactly two rest carriers, **skeleton breath and status-dot pulse**, both compositor-only, both off under reduced motion, both breathing on an ease (never a linear conveyor). Breath of life here means state-change springs and rest carriers, never hover—K13's falsifier stands. The cut: five arithmetic laws replace thirteen literals, four C-severity paint defects close, one two-major-old cross-repo relay gets written, and 994 lines of gate that asserted none of it collapse to ~205 that do. The headline is D1—no DAG row, one bench, two majors of silence.

## 3 · §THE DESIGN

**3.1 The register law.** One declaration set, `src/styles/glass/mark.css` (subtractive—replaces six ad-hoc fragments): `backdrop-filter: none` stated as law; zero `:hover` arms (K13); one ink; one stadium spelling; engagement = `springPreset("snappy")` on state change (StatusDot state, Badge tone)—values read from `springPresets.ts` at implementation time, never a remembered literal—plus the two rest carriers, both breathing on an ease. Everything else is still, and the stillness is the design.

**3.2 Five laws replace thirteen literals.**

| member | law | value |
|---|---|---|
| avatar box | sm IS the control rung; md/lg are φ, φ² of it—box not type, so riding `--ui-scale` is legal per §1.1 | `var(--control-h-md)` · `calc(… * 1.618)` · `calc(… * 2.618)` → 40/64.7/104.7 desktop, 60/97/157 coarse; only `lg` moves (128→104.7; md was already φ·sm within 1%); default stays `sm`; collapse impossible by construction |
| avatar status slot | slot = avatar/4, **via the font-size seam** (S15/D34) | slot sets `font-size: calc(var(--avatar-size)/2)`; the em law does the rest → 10/16/26; the fixed `0.875rem` slot dies |
| status-dot box | the mark rides the line it sits in | `0.5em/0.625em/0.875em`—byte-identical at root 16, now tracks; K17 cannot recur silently |
| badge inset + type | a badge is an annotation, not a control—§1.4 rungs by ROLE, pad on the series, off the scalar (§1.1 mobile law verified) | pad 4/8 · 4/12 · 8/12; type caption (n=−3) · section-label (n=−2) · control-value (n=−1); ratio φ^(1/4) asserted as ratio—pixel values are the typography wave's (its caption computes 14.384 vs canonical 12.97, routed) |
| badge + dot glyph | W-SEARCH ruling 3 inherited verbatim | `1em`; the `--ui-glyph-sm` badge reader dies |
| label type | PROPORTION §6's budgeted `--control-label` | `calc(var(--control-text) * 0.886653)`—0.887 at every viewport and pointer class by construction; Label its first consumer |
| divider ink | §1.2 + the gap law; `--separator-ink` DIES (zero external consumers, re-verified) | gap ≥32 → no line; gap 20 → `--ink-seam` 0.08 (Separator in-content, Select/DropdownMenu/Command separators); flush plate edge → `--ink-edge` 0.16; `.dock-separator` already K10's—named, not re-struck |

**3.3 Separator.** `:112` `block-size:100%` → delete + `align-self: stretch` (A7's banked cure, 1×0→1×22.97); `.separator-labelled { block-size: auto }`; segment `min-inline-size` 16→12; label leaves mono for the sans section-label rung. Sibling DOM and reka semantics untouched—the paint folds, the a11y does not.

**3.4 Skeleton—F24 closed at its mechanism (S2).** The travelling band dies entirely (`::after` gradient, `skeleton-scan`, `will-change`, the `--duration-shimmer-fast` reader—which survives for its other consumers—and the no-op transparency branch):

```css
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference) {
  .skeleton { animation: skeleton-breathe 1.1s var(--ease-standard) infinite alternate; }
}
@keyframes skeleton-breathe { to { opacity: 0.55; } }
@media (forced-colors: active) { .skeleton { animation: none; } }
```

Identical on a 213px chip and a 1246px block; opacity is compositor-only; `alternate` + the canon ease carry the weight a linear conveyor cannot; motion is 100% of the cycle vs 71% dead. The `@layer` radius seam and its comment survive verbatim. The 1.1s clock's rank-table conformance routed to MOTION-CANON.

**3.5 Badge.** (1) Stamp RESOLVED axes from the same defaulting the class map applies. (2) One edge mechanism: outline's `border` dies → `box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--ink-perimeter)`; the contrast arm takes the same inset spelling; per-mode arms only, never inside `light-dark()` ([[feedback-lightdark-inset-shadow]]); the false comment becomes true. (3) Rim declared locally, top leg ≤0.12—the ambient `--glass-material-rim` read dies; the fork itself is W-GLASS-DEDUP's. (4) `white-space: nowrap`; `wrap-anywhere` dies. (5) `variants.ts` breaks the cycle. (6) `surface` axis + the `SURFACE` map + `glass-atom.css:129-163` + `--badge-glass-strength` DELETED on the register law (census and the broken tint map corroborate; the circularity objection is answered by the law). Relay to sci-report routed.

**3.6 StatusDot (S3 composed).** (1) `.feedback-mark`'s base default re-points to the **unknown** paint—an unhandled state can never again ship as live. (2) `[data-state="active"]` gains its explicit binding (`--info`/`--info-foreground`) **and** its resting silhouette: the orbit (`inset:-30%`, 1px ring, opacity 0.28) at every motion value—the silhouette is its own pulse, frozen; `[data-motion]` animates it (1.8s ease-out kept—a breath, not a traverse); the reduced-motion arm collapses to `animation: none`; a forced-colors arm generalizes the existing one. Seven distinct silhouettes incl. monochrome/forced-colors; README:3 becomes true and the README is corrected, not deleted (S10). (3) `50%` ×4 → `var(--radius-pill)` (warning 18%/error 22% shape radii exempt as silhouette geometry, enumerated). (4) `feedback.ts`→`states.ts`; `FeedbackSize`→`StatusDotSize` at source; `cn()` merge. (5) Root barrel gains status-dot; `./status-dot` kept. (6) **The MIGRATION rows are this wave's deliverable**: `variant/color/pulse`→`state`+`--feedback-state-color`; `size="xs"`→`sm`; `label` visible-text→`aria-label` (the headline row); `.status-dot__{dot,pulse,fill}` deletion.

**3.7 Label + host contract.** The four hand-rolled destructures → `fixedHostAttrs`; the Avatar/Skeleton strips SURVIVE as documented contract fences with one-line comments naming `MIGRATION.md:118/121` (S8). Label: `font-size: var(--control-label)`; destructive requirement arm struck (annotation `--muted-foreground` on both arms); "optional" enters the a11y tree, `*` stays hidden, the control-side `aria-required` fence routed as a named obligation (S13); disabled = ink 0.45 + chroma 0 at full geometry α; `margin-inline-start` 0.35em→4px. Avatar: layer split per Skeleton's model (shape seam only layered); `.glass-avatar`→`.avatar` and `.glass-label`→`.label` (clean break; named sweep: `demo/stories/data/avatar.vue`, `label.contract`, `avatar.contract`, `w1-radius-redress{,.webkit}.spec.ts`, `_capture_css.spec.ts`); border `--glass-border-wash`→`--ink-edge` (C9—elevation earns the edge); specular untouched in-lane (S6—PROPORTION 5b's, library-wide); `data-image-state`/`loadingStatusChange`/`AvatarImageStatus` deleted (0 readers, six repos); `delay-ms` forwarded (−2 lines).

**3.8 Demo.** `badge.vue` mounts `<StatusDot :state>` in place of the hand-rolled dots—kills 8 `calc(infinity*1px)` corners, the viz-palette-as-semantics row, 4 dead specimens, and makes its own blurb true (F14). `separator.vue` twins fold to one plate, a11y delta in copy. `status-dot.vue` captions stop out-sizing specimens (K17). `avatar.vue:155,231` + `status-dot.vue:185` are LAYOUT kill #13's (arm-inverted per T-B3)—consumed, not re-solved; the `:deep()` rim override dies with the layer split.

**Public surface after:** one prop (`Badge.surface`) + one emit (`loadingStatusChange`) deleted, zero added; +1 barrel line; `delay-ms` restored; MIGRATION rows added. Both foremen looked hard for a bigger prop diet and found none; this spec declines to manufacture one.

**ADD**—`align-self: stretch` · the labelled real block box · resolved-axis stamps · the inset-ring edge · `nowrap` · `--control-label` consumption · the announced "optional" · the neutral unhandled default + the `active` rule + the orbit silhouette · `skeleton-breathe` · the φ ladder + font-size slot seam · em dot rungs · `1em` glyphs · `fixedHostAttrs` ×4 + 2 named contract comments · `delay-ms` forwarding · +1 barrel line · the MIGRATION StatusDot rows · `<StatusDot>` mounted in `badge.vue` · state-change springs · `mark.css` (~25, subtractive of six fragments). **Net new machinery: zero. Net new public API: one barrel line.**

## 5 · §GATES—four born-RED + folds; 994 → ~205 (−789)

| # | gate | assertion | RED at HEAD | mutation that bites |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| G1 MARK (π, both engines) | ∀ mark: `backdropFilter==='none'` · zero `:hover` rules match · radius ∈ {pill, 0} (enumerated shape exemptions) · outline height ≡ filled height per size · every `[data-slot=badge]` carries RESOLVED `data-variant/tone/size` · 7 distinct silhouette hashes incl. forced-colors · `[data-state=active]` matches an explicit rule with a resting `::after` | 8 infinity-px + 4 `50%`; +2.00 ×3; 22/39 stamps missing; `active` ≡ unhandled default | restore `rounded-full` on one demo dot → RED in both engines, 20 orders apart; any edge occupying layout box → RED; raw-prop stamp → RED |
| G2 EXTENT (π) | every `role="separator"`/`data-slot="separator"` node: both rect dims > 0; any text child's rect contained by the host's | 1×0 ×2; 1246×1 vs 18.7px label | restoring `block-size:100%` or the clamp → RED; wrapper-proof (asserts on the role-bearing node) |

## 3 · §THE DESIGN

### 3.1 The groove — one register, one token, one arithmetic

`.glass-track-well` keeps its name (namespace, not material claim — `.glass-value-marks` carries it identically; two landed gates pin the string) and sheds the dead paint: `background: var(--muted-medium)` and its six sanctioning comment lines strike; three declarations survive (`position/overflow/border-radius`).

**One token, `--track-well-recess`**, declared once, its value the law itself: `oklch(from var(--background) calc(l * 0.96) c h)` — host-relative by unregistered-custom-property substitution (ruling 5), dark mode derives free, both literal arms retire. Plus one top-only inset ink edge at **α 0.16** (a recess has no top light — A10's clause; GF-TIMELINE's identical spelling), plain per-mode arms if a dark override proves needed (never inside `light-dark()`; dark arm on a plain `.dark` ancestor). Progress's rail: `background: var(--glass-progress-track-background, var(--track-well-recess))`. The rim's `__track` (`:33`) reads the same token. `--muted-medium` retires as a groove ground in this lane's files; the slider's fallback swap is the banked slider terminal's (§9).

**One typed grammar:** `--glass-progress-track-background` — `background` grammar, the sibling's name shape, still a distinct property (the W4 anti-collision ground preserved). `--glass-progress-track-color` dies at birth, no alias (0 writers, 7 repos); the `<color>` clause and its grep gate die with the `color-mix()` (§3.2).

**One mark recipe, one passed-encoding:** the rim composes `.glass-value-marks`/`.glass-value-mark`, deletes `styles.css:85-116` (−32); register geometry verified compatible (start-origin horizontal, block-end vertical, direction-agnostic `::before` centring). The register gains the family's single "passed" law: `.glass-value-mark[data-consumed] { opacity: 0; }` with one transition on the dock pair — set by every composer when `mark.position <= fraction` (ruling 9; the rim's `SWALLOW_BAND` fractional math and inline opacity die, ~8 lines). Diameter: the shipped `0.375rem` default **stands unchanged** — no law minted (ruling 4). `50%` → `--radius-pill` at both surviving dot sites. The rim's `__track` does **not** compose the well (ruling 10 — the clip falsifier); its genuine divergences (absolute position, per-orientation insets, clip-path reveal) stay scoped; the D-16 record is replaced by this one-line ground.

### 3.2 Indeterminate — one law, one door, one clock

- **The door.** `indeterminate` prop **deleted**; `modelValue?: number | null`, default `0`; explicit `null` = indeterminate — reka's own contract (ruling 2). `data-indeterminate` survives as the CSS hook (the shared partial serves non-reka hosts). Marks under indeterminate: emptied silently; the `throw` watchEffect dies whole.
- **The carrier.** New **`src/styles/glass/track-flow.css`** (~30-38 lines, `@layer components`, `@import`-ed in `glass.css` beside its three sibling registers — W7 no-orphan discipline): the specular flow band on `::after`, clipped to its host, **`--duration-shimmer-fast` (3s)** — one indeterminate clock in the library — `plus-lighter`, peak α **0.12**, amplitude floor **0.30·peak**, a `background-position` **envelope, never a spring** (ruling 6); **PRM parks it at 50% at floor alpha — the state survives at reduced amplitude.** Composed by Progress and Timeline; available to the rim.
- **Deleted with the fork:** both gradient arms (`:166-197`), `> * { display: none }` (`:199-201`), both keyframe families (`:245-261`), the PRM arms (`:263-279` partial — S3 dies with its cause, both orientations), `--motion-duration-progress-indeterminate`.
- **The inherited falsifier:** kill the flow and `modelValue: null` is pixel-identical to `modelValue: 0`.
- **Boundary record:** Progress is Ruling A's second lawful host — the non-interactive reporting substrate with nothing else in the box, strengthening GF-TIMELINE's most strikeable clause. **Zero amendments owed in either direction**; its Ruling C/§6 adopted verbatim; recorded so neither lane re-derives.

### 3.3 Travel, size, fill, completion, announcement

- **Travel:** `:140` → `transition: transform var(--spring-dock-duration) var(--spring-dock)`; rim `:59-60` (clip-path reveal) and the register's swallow crossfade → the same pair; the `0.44s`/`ease-out` literals die (M4 class). Discharges canon `:83` on both carriers. No meniscus, no swell, no overshoot (dock monotone, max 0.97712 — measured). Stated limitation: a continuously-fed bar trails its announced value by ≤ the 0.22s settle; the story copy says so; π4 measures on a discrete step only.
- **Size:** `size?: "sm" | "md" | "lg"` → `data-size`, block-size **8/12/20** (`0.75rem` md default = the family's shared rung), mobile transposes one rung down. Vertical thickness rides the same axis; vertical length inlines **`12rem`** (ruling 8). Scoped size rule stays unlayered (sizing is component business, `track-well.css:22-26`'s own ruling). `--progress-size`/`--progress-vertical-size` die; the demo's `h-1.5`/`h-6`/`h-7` die and the `sizes` section binds the prop.
- **Fill:** `--progress-fill` **survives** as the role-named knob, bridged exactly as the slider (`:152` stays: `--liquid-fill-tint: var(--progress-fill, …)`); default **stays `--primary`** (ruling 3 — CR 12.52 vs capsule-warm's 1.03); error arm unchanged (`:122` + the A9 ring at `:123`). The de-shadcn is deferred to the token lane with the constraint stated: any replacement clears **≥3:1 against `--track-well-recess`, both modes, per host** — T-FILL-1 pins it.
- **Completion (breath edict):** the discharge glow becomes **variant-independent** — one-shot at fraction 1.0, box-shadow light pulse capped at the **0.12** specular ceiling (from 95%), on `--duration-normal var(--ease-standard)` (an EFFECTS leg — lawful, ruling 7). Intake pulse + crescendo + `background-blend-mode: screen` struck; `variant` narrows to `"default" | "liquid"`; PRM drops the glow (end state carries the fact).
- **Announcement (reka-native):** `valueText?: string`; bound via reka's own **`:get-value-text`** — determinate `valueText ?? "N%"` (rounded), indeterminate `valueText ?? "in progress"`, error `"failed at N%"`; `modelValue` rounded to ≤2dp before reka (the raw-float forward dies); `aria-orientation` deleted (`:78`); the story's unnamed bars named; `modelValue` destructured out of `delegatedProps`.

### 3.4 Public surface

```ts
// progress/types.ts
export type ProgressVariant = "default" | "liquid";   // "gradient" folds
export type ProgressStatus = "default" | "error";
export type ProgressSize = "sm" | "md" | "lg";        // NEW
export interface ProgressProps extends PrimitiveProps {
    modelValue?: number | null;  // null = indeterminate (reka's own door); default 0
    max?: number;                // default 100
    class?: HTMLAttributes["class"];
    variant?: ProgressVariant;
    status?: ProgressStatus;
    orientation?: Orientation;   // from _shared/axes
    size?: ProgressSize;         // NEW
    marks?: readonly number[];   // emptied under indeterminate, silently
    valueText?: string;          // NEW — feeds reka getValueText
}
```

`scroll-progress-rim/types.ts` becomes the one type home: `ScrollProgressRimProps` moves out of the SFC; `Orientation` imports from `_shared/axes` (the duplicate union dies); the zero-content `ScrollProgressRimSegments` alias inlines as `readonly number[]`; `index.ts` re-points (a public-surface break: two type exports drop, booked §8).

## 7 · §REJECTED — falsifiers on record, do not re-raise

- **"`variant=liquid` is dead in paint" (Fable-foreman S5)** — the route mounts `StorySection` (no glass class); `material.css:66`'s subtree `none` requires a `.glass-*` ancestor; the 3 non-`none` surfaces in the live census are the liquid fills themselves.
- **Keep the `indeterminate` boolean / strike the null door (Opus-foreman)** — reka types `modelValue: [Number, null]` and derives `progressState` from nullish; `:69` translates the boolean into null today; both doors are unit-tested so neither is dead — redundancy resolves toward the substrate's door. Zero consumers, zero churn.
- **Derive indeterminate from omission** — Progress has no phase model; omission is `0`. Re-mints the fabricated-value lie Ruling C struck twice.
- **Default the fill to `--glass-capsule-warm`** — CR ≈1.03:1 against the groove, computed twice (live seat + relative-luminance re-derivation this seat). Invisible.
- **Collapse `--progress-fill` into `--liquid-fill-tint`** — forks the family's on-disk blessed shape (`Slider.vue`'s "PRESERVED, mapped onto" bridge), which is load-bearing: `--slider-range-bg` has a live consumer at `PlaybackRibbon.vue:232`.
- **Any mark-diameter law** — `0.5×h` mints 10 off the `4·8·12·20·32·52` generator (R27's class); the tick-rung citation over-reaches PROPORTION `:230`'s own scope column ("the checkbox. Nothing else"). The shipped default stands; zero bytes.
- **The flow traverse rides `springPreset("dock")`** — GF-TIMELINE `:136` verbatim names `background-position` on `--duration-shimmer-fast`; canon REST `:185`: "envelope, not spring — a slow LFO." Dock is travel's.
- **Directory-wide ban on `--ease-standard`/`--duration-normal`** — canon `:192` puts `--ease-standard` on EFFECTS legs by name; the ban would outlaw the canon's own grammar (and `liquid-fill.css:63-65` lawfully rides it). Travel legs only.
- **Container-driven vertical length** — a bare vertical mount collapses to zero (the proposing spec's own admission); the inline `12rem` is paint-neutral against the demo's `h-48`.
- **Preserve the persist-vs-swallow mark distinction** — it never painted: `:133`'s `z-index: 1` occludes passed marks under the opaque default fill; the "distinction" was a variant-dependent z-index accident. One declared encoding.
- **Rename `.glass-track-well` → `.track-well`** — the prefix is the register namespace (`.glass-value-marks` carries it declaring zero glass); `typed-track-seam.test.ts:49-55` forbids un-namespaced generics; `track-well-fold.test.ts:19,29` pins the string. PROPORTION 5a's census clause is the correct fix (routed).
- **Rim composes `.glass-track-well`** — dots are children of `__track`; the register's `overflow: hidden` clips 6px dots on the 4px band. Verified geometry, visible break.
- **A literal-valued `--track-well-recess` / per-consumer derivation copies** — a literal cannot be host-relative; a derivation copied per consumer re-forks the law. The one token holding the derivation is both.
- **Merge Progress into Timeline · delete Progress on the zero-consumer census · `@property` for the typed input · backdrop-filter on the groove · meniscus/swell on a single-span meter · an overshooting fill spring (snappy peaks 1.03153, measured) · an engine fork for `plus-lighter`** — all refuted as in both feeder specs; falsifiers carried forward unchanged.

## §2 · COLLISIONS — one owner per file per cut, RULED (checked on disk)

**C-1 · THE TRACK SEAM — progress vs surface-material, direct contradiction, RULED: eviction + rename WINS (surface-material).** Surface-material R-G evicts `track-well.css`→`styles/track-well.css`, class→`.track-well`; progress refuses, citing the two landed gates that pin the string. Disk: the class declares zero glass (`position/overflow/border-radius/background: var(--muted-medium)`); the batch applies the "glass- prefix promises a material" law **three times independently** (display-atoms D29 renames `.glass-avatar`/`.glass-label`; surface-material G-SM3; progress itself renames `--glass-progress-track-color`→`-background` on the same honesty axis)—progress is the lone holdout and its own falsifier (".glass-value-marks carries the prefix identically") is scoped away by leaving value-marks untouched this cut. The pinned gates amend in the same cut per progress's own §4.0 discipline (`typed-track-seam.test.ts:42` dies with the background strike regardless; `track-well-fold.test.ts:19,29` re-pin the new string). **Everything else in progress's seam survives verbatim on the renamed class**: `--track-well-recess` derivation, background strike, `--glass-progress-track-background` grammar, `track-flow.css`. Owners: surface-material = the file move; progress = the paint/grammar edits; ONE joint cut. G-SM3's law gains two enumerated carve-outs: light-channel registers (`track-flow`'s plus-lighter specular) and the pending-rename value-marks pair.

**C-2 · `material-roles.css` — card edits :20-22 vs surface-material DELETES the file. RULED: DELETE (surface-material owns).** Deletion cures card's D1 a fortiori—no substitution rule can exist. Card's G-CARD-RIM re-keys to "default card resolves the rung's full multi-leg stack" (same born-RED, same GREEN); card's cast leg (`--shadow-card`) moves into `card/styles.css` as an APPENDED leg—the composition card wanted, in card's own file. RULED: split by halves, the class DIES.** Disk shows `surfaces.css:48-90` is a material recipe + a `:has(:focus-visible)` focus-elevation register in one class. **C-5 · THE FIELD REGISTER — forms-seam's six-member `.field-control` parity set vs binary-triad's `.control-bit` (which strikes `.glass-control-edge`/`.control-surface` off the checkbox) vs toggle-group's TF-9 (adds `glass-control-edge` to items). RULED: two registers, one token law.** `.field-control` = line/block text surfaces, **5 members** (input, textarea, nf-input, tags-input, select-trigger—checkbox leaves forms' G-F1 byte-parity clause: a 20px r6 face cannot share padding/radius with a 40px r10 field); `.control-bit` = the bits.
